      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro)
                          WHERE(Num'ro=FILTER(1..));
        DataItemVarName=DimensionLoop1;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF NOT ShowInternalInfo THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           IF Num'ro = 1 THEN BEGIN
                             IF NOT PostedDocDim1.FIND('-') THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;
                           END ELSE
                             IF NOT Continue THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;

                           CLEAR(DimText);
                           Continue := FALSE;
                           REPEAT
                             OldDimText := DimText;
                             IF DimText = '' THEN
                               DimText := STRSUBSTNO(
                                 '%1 %2',PostedDocDim1."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim1."Dimension Value Code")
                             ELSE
                               DimText :=
                                 STRSUBSTNO(
                                   '%1, %2 %3',DimText,
                                   PostedDocDim1."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim1."Dimension Value Code");
                             IF STRLEN(DimText) > MAXSTRLEN(OldDimText) THEN BEGIN
                               DimText := OldDimText;
                               Continue := TRUE;
                               EXIT;
                             END;
                           UNTIL (PostedDocDim1.NEXT = 0);
                         END;

        DataItemLinkReference=Purch. Inv. Header;
      }

                           //      message('%1,%2', "Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection recept No.","Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection Recept Line");
                               IF NOT  recPostReceipLinePrestations_G.GET(recPostReceipLinePrestations_G."Receipt Type"::Receipt,
                                 "Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection recept No.","Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection Recept Line") THEN BEGIN

                               END ELSE BEGIN

                               END;
                             END ELSE BEGIN
                               Line_L := "Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection Recept Line";
                             END;

                             IF recPostReceipLine_G.GET(recPostReceipLine_G."Receipt Type"::Receipt,"Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection recept No.",
                               Line_L) THEN BEGIN

                           //    message('qte : %1',recPostReceipLine_G.Quantity);
                               G_Qt'Livr'es:=recPostReceipLine_G.Quantity;
                               G_Qt'brutes:= recPostReceipLine_G."Brut Quantity";
                               // ESR-7
                           //-->20141219-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-8 Before
                           //    IF ((Type =Type::Item) AND ("Line Collection Type" =  "Line Collection Type"::Collection )) THEN BEGIN
                           //<--20141219-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-8 After
                              IF ("Line Collection Type" =  "Line Collection Type"::Collection ) THEN BEGIN

                           //  G_Qt'brutes:= G_Qt'brutes +recPostReceipLine_G."Brut Quantity";
                             END;
                                 IF (( Type=Type::"G/L Account" )AND
                                   (("Line Collection Type" <>  "Line Collection Type"::Tax) AND
                                   ("Line Collection Type" <>  "Line Collection Type"::"Fixe Storage")
                                    )) OR ( Type=Type::Item) THEN BEGIN
                                  G_MTTOt += "Line Amount";
                               END;
                           //<--20140604 ESR PB Qte Brute
                             // ESR-5
                             IF(Bonprec_G <> "Collection recept No." ) OR ( Ligprec_G <> Line_L ) THEN BEGIN
                               BoolMemeBonLigne_G  :=FALSE;
                               Bonprec_G  :=  "Collection recept No." ;
                               Ligprec_G := Line_L ;
                             END ELSE BEGIN
                               BoolMemeBonLigne_G  :=TRUE;
                             END;

                             IF CollectReceipts_G."Actual Location Code" > ''   THEN BEGIN
                               Wmag_G :=CollectReceipts_G."Actual Location Code";
                             END ELSE BEGIN
                               Wmag_G :=CollectReceipts_G."Location Code";
                             END;

                               CLEAR(TablePostWeighingWeighbridge_G);

                             // --> 20130111-Cer07-CMC02.01.00-MMI
                             //BEFORE
                             //recPostReceipLine_G.GET(recPostReceipLine_G."Receipt Type"::Receipt,"Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection recept No.",
                             //  "Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection Recept Line");

                            // ESR-8 IF "Purch. Inv. Header"."Collection Invoice Type" = "Purch. Inv. Header"."Collection Invoice Type"::Supplement THEN BEGIN
                            // ESR-8 recPostReceipLinePrestations_G.GET(recPostReceipLine_G."Receipt Type"::Receipt,"Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection recept No.",
                            // ESR-8 "Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection Recept Line");
                            // ESR-8 recPostReceipLine_G.GET(recPostReceipLine_G."Receipt Type"::Receipt,"Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection recept No.",

                             G_recPostAnalyse.SETRANGE("Receipt No.","Collection recept No.");

                                 RecAverageReceiptAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Receipt Type",RecAverageReceiptAnalysis_L."Receipt Type"::Receipt);

                                 RecAverageReceiptAnalysis_L.SETRANGE("Line No.","Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection Recept Line");

                                      "Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection recept No.","Purch. Inv. Line"."Collection Recept Line",
                                      G_recPostAnalyse."Analysis Code")
                                 THEN BEGIN

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=4;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro)
                          WHERE(Num'ro=FILTER(1..));
        DataItemVarName=DimensionLoop2;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF NOT ShowInternalInfo THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;

                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Purch. Inv. Line");
                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Document No.","Purch. Inv. Line"."Document No.");
                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Line No.","Purch. Inv. Line"."Line No.");
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           IF Num'ro = 1 THEN BEGIN
                             IF NOT PostedDocDim2.FIND('-') THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;
                           END ELSE
                             IF NOT Continue THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;

                           CLEAR(DimText);
                           Continue := FALSE;
                           REPEAT
                             OldDimText := DimText;
                             IF DimText = '' THEN
                               DimText := STRSUBSTNO(
                                 '%1 %2',PostedDocDim2."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim2."Dimension Value Code")
                             ELSE
                               DimText :=
                                 STRSUBSTNO(
                                   '%1, %2 %3',DimText,
                                   PostedDocDim2."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim2."Dimension Value Code");
                             IF STRLEN(DimText) > MAXSTRLEN(OldDimText) THEN BEGIN
                               DimText := OldDimText;
                               Continue := TRUE;
                               EXIT;
                             END;
                           UNTIL (PostedDocDim2.NEXT = 0);
                         END;

      }
      SECTIONS
      {
      }

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro);
        DataItemVarName=VATCounterLCY;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF (NOT GLSetup."Print VAT specification in LCY") OR
                           ("Purch. Inv. Header"."Currency Code"  = '') OR
                           (VATAmountLine.GetTotalVATAmount = 0) THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;

                        SETRANGE(Num'ro,1,VATAmountLine.COUNT);
                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS(VALVATBaseLCY,VALVATAmountLCY);

                        IF GLSetup."LCY Code" = '' THEN
                          VALSpecLCYHeader := Text007 + Text008
                        ELSE
                          VALSpecLCYHeader := Text007 + FORMAT(GLSetup."LCY Code");

                        CurrExchRate.FindCurrency("Purch. Inv. Header"."Posting Date","Purch. Inv. Header"."Currency Code",1);
                        CalculatedExchRate := ROUND(1 / "Purch. Inv. Header"."Currency Factor" * CurrExchRate."Exchange Rate Amount",0.000001);
                        VALExchRate := STRSUBSTNO(Text009,CalculatedExchRate,CurrExchRate."Exchange Rate Amount");
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           VATAmountLine.GetLine(Num'ro);

                           VALVATBaseLCY := ROUND(VATAmountLine."VAT Base" / "Purch. Inv. Header"."Currency Factor");
                           VALVATAmountLCY := ROUND(VATAmountLine."VAT Amount" / "Purch. Inv. Header"."Currency Factor");
                         END;

      }

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro)
                          WHERE(Num'ro=CONST(1));
        DataItemVarName=Total;
        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           G_recCompEntr.RESET;
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("SettOff Type",G_recCompEntr."SettOff Type"::Posted);
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Invoice No","Purch. Inv. Header"."No.");
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Entry Type",G_recCompEntr."Entry Type"::Customer);
                           G_recCompEntr.CALCSUMS(Amount);
                           G_MttComp:=G_recCompEntr.Amount;

                           G_recCompEntr.RESET;
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("SettOff Type",G_recCompEntr."SettOff Type"::Posted);
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Invoice No","Purch. Inv. Header"."No.");
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Entry Type",G_recCompEntr."Entry Type"::Vendor);
                           G_recCompEntr.CALCSUMS(Amount);
                           G_MttFourLett:=G_recCompEntr.Amount;

                           G_recCompEntr.RESET;
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("SettOff Type",G_recCompEntr."SettOff Type"::Posted);
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Invoice No","Purch. Inv. Header"."No.");
                           G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Entry Type",G_recCompEntr."Entry Type"::Loan);
                           G_recCompEntr.CALCSUMS(Amount);
                           G_MttDEtte:=G_recCompEntr.Amount;

                           //WSA
                           CalcAmountForEDLC_G := "Purch. Inv. Line"."Amount Including VAT"-G_MttComp-G_MttFourLett-G_MttDEtte;
                         END;

      }

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table123;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Document No.,Line No.);
        DataItemVarName=ReceptLine;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        // debut ajout esl 20160609   enlever de la propriet' de l'item " DataItemTableView " de "receptline"
                        // avant "Line Collection Type"="Line Collection Type"::Collection
                        IF ("Line Collection Type"="Line Collection Type"::Collection) OR ("Line Collection Type"="Line Collection Type"::Supplement)
                        THEN BEGIN
                        END ELSE BEGIN
                        CurrReport.SKIP;
                        END;
                        // fin ajout esl 20160609
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=VAR
                           L_i@1000000003 : Integer;
                         BEGIN
                           // debut ajout esl 20160609
                           IF ("Line Collection Type"="Line Collection Type"::Collection)   THEN BEGIN
                           // fin ajout esl 20160609
                           IF NOT recPostReceipLine_G.GET(recPostReceipLine_G."Receipt Type"::Receipt,"Collection recept No.",
                              "Collection Recept Line") THEN CurrReport.SKIP;

                           IF NOT CollectReceipts_G.GET(CollectReceipts_G."Receipt Type"::Receipt,ReceptLine."Collection recept No.") THEN
                            CollectReceipts_G.INIT;
                           END;

                           //R'sultats d'analyse
                           CLEAR(G_ResulAnalyse);
                           G_recPostAnalyse.RESET;
                           G_recPostAnalyse.SETCURRENTKEY(G_recPostAnalyse."Sort Order");
                           G_recPostAnalyse.SETRANGE("Receipt Type",G_recPostAnalyse."Receipt Type"::Receipt);
                           G_recPostAnalyse.SETRANGE("Receipt No.","Collection recept No.");
                           // ajout esl 20160609
                             G_recPostAnalyse.SETFILTER("Sort Order", '< 100');
                           // FIN AJUT esr 20160609
                           IF G_recPostAnalyse.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
                             L_i:=1;
                             REPEAT
                               G_ResulAnalyse[L_i]:=G_recPostAnalyse.Value;
                                G_TitreAnaLyse[L_i]:=FORMAT(G_recPostAnalyse."Analysis Code");
                                 IF STRLEN(G_TitreAnaLyse[L_i])>3 THEN
                                   G_TitreAnaLyse[L_i] := COPYSTR(G_TitreAnaLyse[L_i],1,3);

                               L_i:=L_i+1;
                             UNTIL G_recPostAnalyse.NEXT=0;
                           END;
                           //****
                         END;

        DataItemLinkReference=Purch. Inv. Header;
        DataItemLink=Document No.=FIELD(No.);

  <Code>Public Function BlankZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value = 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankZeroAndPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNeg(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNegAndZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function
REM Reset Page Number:

Shared offset as Integer
Shared newPage as Object
Shared currentgroup1 as Object
Shared currentgroup2 as Object
Shared currentgroup3 as Object

Public Function GetGroupPageNumber(NewPage as Boolean, pagenumber as Integer) as Object
  If NewPage
    offset = pagenumber - 1
  End If
  Return pagenumber - offset
End Function

Public Function IsNewPage(group1 as Object, group2 as Object, group3 as Object) As Boolean
newPage = FALSE
If Not (group1 = currentgroup1)
    newPage = TRUE
    currentgroup1 = group1
    currentgroup2 = group2
    currentgroup3 = group3
     ELSE 
      If Not (group2 = currentgroup2)
        newPage = TRUE 
        currentgroup2 = group2
        currentgroup3 = group3
        ELSE
          If Not (group3 = currentgroup3)
          newPage = TRUE 
          currentgroup3 = group3
         End If
     End If
  End If
Return newPage
End Function
Shared Data1 as Object
Shared Data2 as Object
Shared Data3 as Object

Public Function GetData(Num as Integer, Group as integer) as Object
if Group = 1 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data1),Chr(177))))
End If

if Group = 2 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data2),Chr(177))))
End If

if Group = 3 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data3),Chr(177))))
End If
End Function

Public Function SetData(NewData as Object,Group as integer)
  If Group = 1 and NewData &gt; "" Then
      Data1 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 2 and NewData &gt; "" Then
      Data2 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 3 and NewData &gt; "" Then
      Data3 = NewData
  End If
End Function

Shared PictureData1 as Object
Shared PictureData2 as Object
Shared PictureData3 as Object


Public Function GetPicture(Group as integer) as Object
if Group = 1 then
   Return PictureData1

if Group = 2 then
   Return PictureData2
End If

if Group = 3 then
   Return PictureData3
End If
End Function

  End If
End Function</Code>
